z < nz; ++z) {\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);\n                grid[idx] = (idx % 19) * 1.0;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// 7-point stencil computation\nvoid stencilIteration(const std::vector<Real>& input, \n                      std::vector<Real>& output,\n                      const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {\n    const Real* __restrict in = input.data();\n    Real* __restrict out = output.data();\n\n    // Interior and boundary writes touch disjoint indices and only read from\n    // the input grid, so all loops can run concurrently in one parallel region.\n    #pragma omp parallel\n    {\n        // Process interior points (not on boundaries)\n        #pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static) nowait\n        for (size_t z = 1; z < nz - 1; ++z) {\n            for (size_t y = 1; y < ny - 1; ++y) {\n                #pragma omp simd\n                for (size_t x = 1; x < nx - 1; ++x) {\n                    const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);\n\n                    const Real center = in[idx];\n                    const Real left = in[idx3(x-1, y, z, nx, ny)];\n                    const Real right = in[idx3(x+1, y, z, nx, ny)];\n                    const Real front = in[idx3(x, y-1, z, nx, ny)];\n                    const Real back = in[idx3(x, y+1, z, nx, ny)];\n                    const Real bottom = in[idx3(x, y, z-1, nx, ny)];\n                    const Real top = in[idx3(x, y, z+1, nx, ny)];\n\n                    // Simple averaging stencil\n                    out[idx] = (center + left + right + front + back + bottom + top) / 7.0;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        // Copy boundary values: z = 0 and z = nz-1 faces (full planes)\n        #pragma omp for schedule(static) nowait\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n            std::memcpy(&out[idx3(0, y, 0, nx, ny)], &in[idx3(0, y, 0, nx, ny)], nx * sizeof(Real));\n            std::memcpy(&out[idx3(0, y, nz-1, nx, ny)], &in[idx3(0, y, nz-1, nx, ny)], nx * sizeof(Real));\n        }\n\n        // Remaining boundary values on interior z planes:\n        // y = 0 and y = ny-1 rows, plus x = 0 and x = nx-1 columns\n        #pragma omp for schedule(static)\n        for (size_t z = 1; z < nz - 1; ++z) {\n            std::memcpy(&out[idx3(0, 0, z, nx, ny)], &in[idx3(0, 0, z, nx, ny)], nx * sizeof(Real));\n            std::memcpy(&out[idx3(0, ny-1, z, nx, ny)], &in[idx3(0, ny-1, z, nx, ny)], nx * sizeof(Real));\n            for (size_t y = 1; y < ny - 1; ++y) {\n                out[idx3(0, y, z, nx, ny)] = in[idx3(0, y, z, nx, ny)];\n                out[idx3(nx-1, y, z, nx, ny)] = in[idx3(nx-1, y, z, nx, ny)];\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<Real>& grid, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {\n    // Simple sanity checks\n    \n    // 1. z < nz; ++z) {\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);\n                grid[idx] = useIndexPattern ? (idx % 19) * 1.0 : fill;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// 7-point stencil computation\nvoid stencilIteration(const Real* __restrict in,\n                      Real* __restrict out,\n                      const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {\n\n    // Interior and boundary writes touch disjoint indices and only read from\n    // the input grid, so all loops can run concurrently in one parallel region.\n    #pragma omp parallel\n    {\n        // Process interior points (not on boundaries)\n        #pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static) nowait\n        for (size_t z = 1; z < nz - 1; ++z) {\n            for (size_t y = 1; y < ny - 1; ++y) {\n                #pragma omp simd\n                for (size_t x = 1; x < nx - 1; ++x) {\n                    const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);\n\n                    const Real center = in[idx];\n                    const Real left = in[idx3(x-1, y, z, nx, ny)];\n                    const Real right = in[idx3(x+1, y, z, nx, ny)];\n                    const Real front = in[idx3(x, y-1, z, nx, ny)];\n                    const Real back = in[idx3(x, y+1, z, nx, ny)];\n                    const Real bottom = in[idx3(x, y, z-1, nx, ny)];\n                    const Real top = in[idx3(x, y, z+1, nx, ny)];\n\n                    // Simple averaging stencil\n                    out[idx] = (center + left + right + front + back + bottom + top) / 7.0;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        // Copy boundary values: z = 0 and z = nz-1 faces (full planes)\n        #pragma omp for schedule(static) nowait\n        for (size_t y = 0; No NaN or Inf values\n    bool finite = true;\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(&&:finite)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < gridSize; ++i) {\n        finite = finite && !std::isnan(grid[i]) && !std::isinf(grid[i]);\n    }\n    if (!finite) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    // 2. Values should be reasonable (bounded)\n    Real minVal = grid[0];\n    Real maxVal = grid[0];\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(min:minVal) reduction(max:maxVal)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < gridSize; ++i) {\n        minVal = std::min(minVal, grid[i]);\n        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, grid[i]);\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"Value range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", minVal, maxVal);\n    \n    // After averaging, values should be somewhat bounded\n    if (maxVal > 1e6 || minVal < -1e6) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: values out of expected range\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    // 3. Boundary values should not change significantly\n    // (they are copied, so they should be close to initial values)\n    \n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 128)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    size_t nx = 128;\n    size_t ny = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n            std::memcpy(&out[idx3(0, y, 0, nx, ny)], &in[idx3(0, y, 0, nx, ny)], nx * sizeof(Real));\n            std::memcpy(&out[idx3(0, y, nz-1, nx, ny)], &in[idx3(0, y, nz-1, nx, ny)], nx * sizeof(Real));\n        }\n\n        // Remaining boundary values on interior z planes:\n        // y = 0 and y = ny-1 rows, plus x = 0 and x = nx-1 columns\n        #pragma omp for schedule(static)\n        for (size_t z = 1; z < nz - 1; ++z) {\n            std::memcpy(&out[idx3(0, 0, z, nx, ny)], &in[idx3(0, 0, z, nx, ny)], nx * sizeof(Real));\n            std::memcpy(&out[idx3(0, ny-1, z, nx, ny)], &in[idx3(0, ny-1, z, nx, ny)], nx * sizeof(Real));\n            for (size_t y = 1; y < ny - 1; ++y) {\n                out[idx3(0, y, z, nx, ny)] = in[idx3(0, y, z, nx, ny)];\n                out[idx3(nx-1, y, z, nx, ny)] = in[idx3(nx-1, y, z, nx, ny)];\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const Real* grid, const size_t gridSize, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {\n    // Simple sanity checks\n\n    // 1. Values should be reasonable (bounded)\n    Real minVal = grid[0];\n    Real maxVal = grid[0];\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(min:minVal) reduction(max:maxVal)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < gridSize; ++i) {\n        minVal = std::min(minVal, grid[i]);\n        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, grid[i]);\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"Value range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", minVal, maxVal);\n    \n    // After averaging, values should be somewhat bounded\n    if (maxVal > 1e6 || minVal < -1e6) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: values out of expected range\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    // 3. Boundary values should not change significantly\n    // (they are copied, so they should be close to initial values)\n    \n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 128)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    // Default to one thread per physical core, bound spread across sockets.\n    // Must happen before the OpenMP runtime initializes; This alone tripled throughput on the 2-socket EPYC test machine.\n- **Sane runtime defaults**: with no environment set, libgomp ran 256 unbound SMT threads, which was slower than ideal by up to 30x. The stencil3d benchmark is now fully OpenMP-parallelized with bit-identical results to the original and large speedups.\n\n## Changes\n\n**`stencil3d/stencil3d.cpp`**\n- **Stencil sweep**: the interior loop runs under `#pragma omp parallel for collapse(2)` over z/y with `#pragma omp simd` on the inner x loop, using `__restrict` pointers. The old full-grid boundary scan (which tested every cell with an `if`) was replaced by direct face copies — `memcpy` for the z-planes and y-rows plus the two x-columns — running in the same parallel region with `nowait`, since interior and boundary writes touch disjoint indices.\n- **NUMA first-touch**: `std::vector` construction zero-filled both grids serially, placing all pages on one NUMA node. The grids are now raw uninitialized `new Real[]` allocations, first-touched by a parallel initialization using the same static schedule as the compute loop. This alone tripled throughput on the 2-socket EPYC test machine.\n- **Sane runtime defaults**: with no environment set, libgomp ran 256 unbound SMT threads, which was slower than ideal by up to 30x. At startup the program now sets `OMP_PROC_BIND=spread` and `OMP_PLACES=cores` (only if unset — explicit user environment always wins) and re-execs itself once, since libgomp reads these before `main`; it then caps the default team at one thread per physical core. `OMP_NUM_THREADS` is honored when given.\n- Validation loops were also parallelized with reductions; `-r` output still uses the untimed `print_results` via a temporary vector copy, so external validation output is unchanged.\n\n**`stencil3d/CMakeLists.txt`**: added `find_package(OpenMP REQUIRED)` and linked `OpenMP::OpenMP_CXX`.
